Action! — Pay-per-view

Blood, Sweat, and Tears, 16 February 2003

16 February 2003
Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Blood, Sweat, and Tears Superdome — New Orleans, Louisiana ---- BLOOD, SWEAT, TEARS. BLOOD, SWEAT, TEARS. What Friends Are For. Starring: LLB & Christopher Fox. (The show opened up to LLB, who once again entered quickly through the Arena doors, speeding down the hallway past everyone in sight.) Liam Ump: And there’s one of the Challengers for the Action Championship tonight, LLB! Derik Shaw: Who’s once again avoiding everyone! (The lawyer turned the corner, as none other than Christopher Fox appeared into view. He smiled, jumping up and running after his best friend.) Christopher Fox: LL! LL! I waited for you! (But ‘The Law’ just ignored his friend, turning the corner and finding his locker room door. Still, though, Christopher keep pace seeming very concerned.) Fox: LL what’s wrong? Do you need to talk to someone? Is everything otay? (Entering through his locker room door, LLB closed it to a very sad Chris Fox.) Fox: Otay... um... well... I will just sit here for now and if you want to talk, we can. (He frowned, lowering himself down the door to sit on the ground below.) Fox: Oh well... ---- Setting Out the Stall. Setting Out the Stall. Starring: The A-Star Stable. (The door opened and light flooded into the room. The switch was flicked and six people walked into the room. ‘Luscious’ Lee Lightning. ‘Daunting’ David Dew. Montrose. Airborn Tyler. Siobhan Tyler Jonny Yankee.) Lee Lightning: Where’s Joey? (He asked as he sat down and flung his bag in a corner. He was, of course, referring to the seventh member of the A-Star Stable, who tonight had a match with Coral Avalon. He’d been traveling with them all week, yet he’d disappeared as soon as they’d arrived at the arena.) Jonny Yankee: I think he went to prepare privately. (Jonny at the moment was nothing more than a passive member of the stable. He’d been nursing a thigh injury for a while now, and there rumors he’d be sent to one of the developmental leagues for rehabilitation. The members quickly went their various ways. Montrose picked up a can of beer from the mini bar and left the room, for he didn’t have a match tonight and he’d already been told by management that he wouldn’t be allowed to accompany The Elements to ringside. Jonny Yankee also made his excuses and left, in search of someone from the medical team. Airborn and Siobhan sat down on the couch, and Airborn put his arm around Siobhan who cuddled up to him. She was looking fine tonight, having straightened her normally shoulder length ginger hair so it extended down to her shoulder blades, and her sparkly purple t-shirt complimented her figure.) Lightning: Give it a break you two. (David sat on the pine bench and unfolded his wrestling tights, before also leaving in search of a road agent. Lee wandered back through from the bathroom and sat down on the couch next to Siobhan.) Lightning: You know, I don’t even know why you’re here. I thought you said you were here to look after our finances? Siobhan Tyler: I am, but a girl’s allowed to have some fun you know. Lightning: Yes, you are. It’d just be nice if you actually looked over our accounts once in a while, seeing as it’s your job and all (Siobhan started to blush as Lee unscrewed the top of his mineral water.) Lightning: Yeah, well, we’ll talk about that later. Lets just have no more screw ups, Airborn? You listening? (Airborn sighed and looked up at the ceiling.) Airborn Tyler: Yeah, I’m listening. Lightning: Good, remember what I said: no unnecessary risks! No jumping from the top of ladders and nearly breaking your neck. Don’t let him anywhere near your ankle. Make quick work of it. Don’t disappoint me. (Lee stood up and also went to leave the room. However, after he set one foot out of the door, he stopped and turned around, pointing at Siobhan.) Lightning: And we all know that with ‘you know what’ still lingering over our heads, you are NOT to interfere in any way shape or form. Stay out of the spotlight. You are not here for fans to goggle at, you are here to stay out of the way and press keys on a calculator. I think we understand each other. (Lee swiveled quickly, flinging his blonde hair through the air before calling down the corridor after David and making his leave. Airborn and Siobhan looked at each other with a sigh of exasperation.) Airborn: Well, we’ve been told. ---- The Ringer vs. Woodchuck Bill The Ringer vs. Woodchuck Bill Ump: Folks our first Match of the night is going to be a Quarter Final Submission Tournament Match! And what a barn burner to kick it off! Shaw: You said it Liam. I mean The Ringer has deservingly waited for that annoying Bill to help him decode that letter, and now it’s time to kick his ass! Ump: Hey that’s not true. Woodchuck’s giving The Ringer an opportunity right here, tonight, to decode the letter himself! Shaw: Oh yeah? How? Ump: If The Ringer wins then Woodchuck will tell him what this letter says. Shaw: Which WILL happen, I assure you. I mean Woodchuck Bill is going to fight a person who has mastered wrestling inside and out! (“Nothing Could Come Between Us” by Theory of a Deadman hit as The Ringer strutted down the ramp, soaked in water and ready to take someone out. Then after the boos died down, he pulled a folded piece of paper out of his tights and grabbed a mic.) The Ringer: Okay, settle down, it’s just me. (The Crowd Booed.) Ump: These people aren’t responding to The Ringer’s annoying tactics. The Ringer: Now tonight, not only will I, the ‘Prototype’ of wrestling proceed one step closer to becoming the ‘Prototype’ of Submission, but I will also destroy Woodchuck Bill and decode this stupid ignorant letter he gave me! So I’ve already taken care of Idiot Number One, aka, Scout Master Scott, but now it’s Idiot Number Two, get out he- Ump: IT’S BILL! From behind the Woodchuck has attacked The Ringer! Shaw: Not fair! Only guys I like do that! **DING DING DING** Ump: The Match has started and the smaller of the two, Woodchuck Bill, has jumped on the back of The Ringer and is now enforcing a tough sleeper hold! Shaw: Come on Ringer! Counter it, counter it! Ump: The Ringer has just grabbed the ropes and thrown himself out of the ring! Shaw: Ha, but Woodchuck held on, and The Ringer landed on his back! Ump: The Crowd certainly heard that! Shaw: Look! Look who’s getting up first! It’s The Ringer baby!! Ump: Ringer just stuffed that letter into Billy’s jean pocket! And now he’s hammering away with furious left hands to the heads! **CRASH!** Ump: Oh my God! The Ringer just tossed Woodchuck Bill into the steel steps and is now throwing him back into the ring! Shaw: Lay it on him Ring-Man! Lay it on! Ump: Ringer is applying an STF submission! The Fans here are responding with tons of boos too! Shaw: Disrespect Liam, that’s what it is! They should praise The Ringer! The Ringer is the ‘Prototype’ of wrestling, the man, he can’t be beat and soon that will sink into Bill’s head when he taps out! Ump: Right now The Ringer is making Bill sweat! Shaw: He should, after all it is ‘Blood, Sweat, Tears’! Hahahaha! Ump: Bill is motioning himself towards the ropes! Can he make it? Shaw: Pull back Ringer! YOU CAN DO IT! Ump: Too late! The Crowd claps as Woodchuck snatches the ropes and Ringer breaks off the move. Shaw: Only to showboat! Yeah show me your biceps baby! Ump: The Ringer was posing while Bill comes in with a low blow! Shaw: Disqualify him Ref! Ump: I don’t think the Referee saw it! Shaw: He had to! Not fair! That’s letting Bill run wild... and I don’t like it! Ump: Left boot to the back, right knee to the lower torso! Ringer is riving in pain here... and lookout!!! Jumping splash connected by Bill! Shaw: Ringer manages to get up and... DAMMIT! He misses with a clothesline! Ump: Bill hits a huge spin kick to the side of the head... and off of the ropes... wham! Leg drop to Ringer! Shaw: These people are loving it! Ump: It’s about to get insane! Bill just jumped up to the top rope that’s the furthest away from The Ringer! What’s he going to do? Shaw: NO! Ump: SHOOTING STAR PRESS MORE THAN HALFWAY ACROSS THE RING! Shaw: Oh my god! I haven’t even seen Chris Fox fly that far! Ump: Now what’s he doing!? Shaw: Oh no, not AGAIN!? Ump: Woodchuck hits a springboard splash!! And now The Ringer’s totally out of it! Shaw: Woodchuck’s waiting for Ringer to get up... and slowly, he will! Ump: He’s coming to his feet... as Bill runs at him and nails a jumping neck breaker! Shaw: Bill’s picking The Ringer up for another move, he pulls him up... ‘The Flagpole’!? ... NO! The Ringer counters with a back body drop as now he measures Bill! Ump: Woody’s getting up... **WHACK!!!** Ump: What a superkick right in the face of Woodchuck Bill and now The Ringer has that look on his face that he’ll do anything to win! Shaw: Sharp... YES!! The Ringer has locked in the sharpshooter! Tap, tap, tap, tap out God dammit! Ump: Wow, I’m surprised The Ringer knows such a move! Shaw: He’s the f’n ‘Prototype’! He can do anything! Ump: Look at this! Woodchuck was reaching back with his right hand as The Ringer has hooked his arm around it! Now not only does he have the sharpshooter on, but one of Bill’s arms too!! Shaw: Innovator! He’s the Innovator of Wrestling!! Ump: Bill’s SO close to those ropes though, that I think The Ringer sees that... and yes, he does! Shaw: What a move by The Ringer! Ump: The Ringer just let go of the sharpshooter and has picked Bill up in that arm submission hold which beat Scout Master Scott and put him out of action for a GOOD while! Shaw: Rip away! RIP THAT AIR TO BITS... BABY!! Ump: Bill’s trying to lower the pain by going on one knee but it doesn’t seem to be working! Shaw: It’s over! All over! Ump: WHAT!? OH MY GOD! WOODCHUCK BILL JUST PICKED UP THE RINGER AND NAILED THE STEEL FIST! HE STOLE THE RINGER’S MOVE! Shaw: NNNNNOOOOO!!! Ump: Bill’s latching on a figure four now and The Ringer seems knocked out! Shaw: Well sure! He landed on his head after all! Ump: The Ref is... (The Fans rise in cheers.) Ump: CALLING FOR THE BELL! IT’S OVER! WOODCHUCK WINS!! BILL WINS IT! A TOTALLY UNTRAINED WRESTLER HAS BEATEN A SO CALLED ‘PROTOTYPE’! Shaw: No! The Ringer never tapped out! Ump: It doesn’t matter! Woodchuck Bill has won! And now what’s this? (Bill pulls the letter out of his pocket and grabs a mic.) Ump: It’s the letter! Woodchuck Bill: Yeeeeepeeeeeeee! I beat you! I win, you lose! Looks like I’m one step closer from getting my submission badge! Hehe! Ump: The Ringer is slowly getting up... he looks extremely mad! Woodchuck: Oh hey Writer, here, hehe, you’ll need this! Ump: Bill has given The Ringer his letter back! The Ringer: Come here and tell me what it says dammit! I want this decoded! Ump: Bill has dropped the mic and is heading backstage! The Ringer: IDIOT!!!! Tell me what this says right now! Shaw: Yeah, tell him! Ump: Bill has left for the backstage! Our cameras will follow him though... The Ringer: Where are you going? Ump: It looks like Woodchuck is heading for the parking lot... what the? Shaw: Is that a moped!? Ump: It appears so! The Ringer: GET BACK HERE!!!!!!!! IDIOT, IDIOT, IDIOT!!!!! Ump: Hey that moped has a Scouting symbol on it! The Ringer: That’s it! I’m coming back there to get you! Ump: The Ringer has left the ring, but it appears to be too late! Woodchuck: Now just you wait Writer! I won so therefore you’ll have to solve the letter on your own. Oh, by the way, happy Valentines day! I got this moped for you! I have one too so maybe we can go sometime! Scout alert! (Bill stood at attention as The Ringer was disgusted.) Woodchuck: Break-off. Ump: Did Bill just give Writer- I mean Ringer a moped? Shaw: Mind games, that’s all it is. Ump: I think Bill was giving Ringer it for a valentines present! (Bill scoots down a hallway and then out of sight, leaving The Ringer confused and angry.) Ump: Oh my! Is that a tag? On the moped? Shaw: Yeah it is Liam, like you really needed to point that out. Ump: It says “From Woodchuck Bill to The Writer”. What a nice gesture! Shaw: Yeah, if you’re a tree hugging environmentalist hippy! Ump: What an eventful Match, as Bill beats The Ringer to move on in the Submission Tournament! (The scene cuts off with The Ringer going up the ramp, saying that one word: “Idiot”.) Outcome: Woodchuck via Submission. ---- Aroma's Blood. Aroma's Blood. Starring: Sweet Aroma. There she stood, in the ring a happy go lucky girl, especially after the two "date rape" attempts. She was ready to do something with her life, college just wasn't her after the second "date rape", she needed something to take away the memories of her past. So she joined up with Hell's Kitchen to learn how to manage. She felt that was something that would help her and Roberto. [Clips of Roberto Aromadeus wrestling in a Hell's Kitchen Wrestling School ring while Veronica is shown on the outside doing her best valet impersonation.] Sweet's stood in the ring at Pressure Point, expecting to wrestle the then-Angelica Dawson, when the music sounded. And out stepped the current-A! Champion, Syphon Fission. She tried to get her shots in. [Footage rolls of Aroma trying to get some offense started on Rashard Clark aka Syphon Fission.] She looks into Fission/Clark's eyes and sees that this isn't about wrestling to him. [Closeup of Aroma's eyes as she sees that Rashard/Syphon isn't here to wrestle.] And down went Aroma. Fission controls the match for the most part until he gets the victory. [Footage of Aroma flat on her back and unconscious.] Fission/Clark stands over Aroma and releases a loogy. [Footage of Aroma laid out with Fission's mucus resting on her person.] Aroma's face then appears on the screen as blood begins to pour down her face. Blood letters then seep onto the screen spelling out... Aroma's Blood. ---- Jordan X Shabazz vs. ??? Jordan X Shabazz vs. ??? As the arena went dark once more, the Blood, Sweat, and Tears logo that appeared on the big screen between matches disappeared, replaced by the same bloodied face that had harkened Duel’s arrival on Pressure Point 49. As the camera watched, a single glob of blood dripped from Duel’s chin, and the camera zoomed in on the claret until it filled the screen. As Nas’ “The Cross” kicked in and a series of video clips highlighting Duel’s career in LCW played on the screen, albeit with a reddish tint and liquidity to them that made them appear as though they were seen through the droplet of blood. Some of the fans started to get up, hoping to sneak a bathroom break in before the next match, but they were halted by an eruption of blue pyrotechnics from the stage. Amidst the sparks stood a man that most of them had seen before, the same masked man who had confronted Jordan Shabazz on the last two Pressure Points leading up to this show, and the same masked man who had beaten Dirk Seller in the opening round of the King of Submissions tournament. As he stood between towers of blue flame, the masked man reached up and loosened the drawstrings on his executioner’s hood. He paused, and many of the fans, frozen in their tracks by the explosion of pyro, settled back into their seats to see what was coming next. Was this masked man really him? Could it be? Ump: The fans are on the edges of their seats here, as they want to know just who exactly this masked man is. He put on a great showing in his match against Dirk Seller this past Pressure Point, and I’m sure he’ll make an excellent addition to the Action roster! As the man loosened the bottom of his hood, the fans, gripped by suspense, fell into a hush as Nas’ voice continued to fill the arena. On the big screen, a clip of Duel’s fall through the roof of a cell with a ladder wrapped around his head was playing, and with one deft motion, the masked man grabbed the top of the hood and whipped it off… Ump: It’s Duel! Duel has come to Action Wrestling, and he’s already wrestled his first match! Shaw: I don’t see the big deal, and I’ve never even heard of this guy. Ump: Duel had been a dominant force in Lethal Championship Wrestling for three years before defecting to their offshoot promotion, Evo, in the fall of 2001! He’s got a tremendous amateur background, having wrestled for Penn State, and is also a veteran of several Japanese tours! Shaw: Well, if he’s the masked man, he’s scheduled to take on the Black Superman, Jordan X Shabazz here tonight, so we’ll see how good he really is. Ump: Indeed we will, Derik, indeed we will…Let’s send it down to Pat Hoskins in the ring for introductions! Hoskins: And our first competitor in this second-round King of Submissions tournament match-up, now unmasked and hailing from Philadelphia, Pennsylvania…standing six feet and three inches tall…weighing two-hundred eighty-three pounds…he…is…Duel! Ump: So he’s the one that beat Dirk Seller in the first round of the tournament! It certainly makes sense now, looking back at it, because of Duel’s storied history in professional wrestling. Shaw: You’re just saying that so you don’t look like an idiot, Liam. Ump: Hey, I never commentated in PIW, so I automatically have more credibility than you, Derik. As Eminem and Sticky Fingaz’ “What If I Was White” blared through the arena, the fans began to boo loudly, clearly not pleased with “the Racist”, Mr. Jordan X Shabazz. As the same single X filled the ActionTron, Shabazz walked to the top of the ramp, his faithful spiritual adviser, the Reverend Jesse Kintei, in tow. Hoskins: And his opponent, from Albany, New York, accompanied by the Reverend Jesse Kintei…standing five feet and eleven inches…weighing two-hundred twenty-eight pounds…he…is...Jordan…X…Shabazz! Ump: This should be quite the contest, as both of these men are relatively new to Action and both are looking to prove something here tonight in the second round of the King of Submissions tournament. Duel advanced with a win over fellow newcomer Dirk Seller, and Shabazz moved forward with a victory over tag team stalwart Joe Russa of the Legion of Dairy 2K3. Shaw: Duel staring down at Shabazz, but Shabazz isn’t backing down at all. I think he’s got this one in the bag, Liam. Ump: We’ll see, Derik – I’ve seen a lot of tapes of Duel in LCW, and some of the things he’s done are downright brutal. There are times where he’s shown no concern for his own well-being, just to further punish his opponent. Shaw: Really? Maybe I’ve been wrong about this guy then… Ump: Either way, this match is ready to begin, and both men have received instruction from the referee and look ready to go here. They move to center of the ring and Shabazz looks ready for Duel’s collar-and-elbow tie-up…but Duel ducks Shabazz’s arms and quickly comes up inside his reach…he’s got Shabazz hooked here – head and arm suplex! Duel wasting no time in gaining the advantage, and he continues to work on Shabazz here. Shaw: Don’t you think it might be a bit premature for this? Ump: I do, Derik, but Duel looking for a crucifix armbar with neck submission nonetheless, and while we saw that this was an effective part of his arsenal in his match against Dirk Seller; apparently, so did Jordan X Shabazz and the Reverend Jess Kintei, who just shouted to let Shabazz know what was coming, and Shabazz is quick to turn and tackle Duel to the mat before the big man could so much as get a single portion of the hold locked in! Shaw: I think Shabazz made the right move in enlisting the reverend, as that bit of tactical advice may’ve been the difference maker in the match. Ump: I think it’s a little early to really call that a difference maker, but regardless, Shabazz is on the offensive, and he’s firing punch after punch into Duel’s face…but wait! Upon closer inspection, Duel is in a guard pose very reminiscent of a shoot fighter, and he’s choosing to simply absorb Shabazz’s blows. Oooh! He caught both of Shabazz’s arms inside of his own, which left “the Racist” wide open for a nasty headbutt from Duel. Ump: With both men now getting back to their feet, it’ll be interesting to see who actually came out of their initial exchange better…Duel charges! Lariat! Shaw: No! Ducked by Shabazz! And he’s got a sleeper hold locked in! Ump: But rather than keeping it locked in, Shabazz has chosen to turn it into a sleeping neck breaker, perhaps in hopes of softening up Duel’s neck and shoulder area for that lethal crossface maneuver he calls the Cracka Crippler. And now it looks like he’s hoping to soften up Duel’s arm, as well, as Shabazz is looking for a cross armbreaker, which is one of the most painful holds in wrestling, and has been imported from shoot-style fighting. Shaw: I’m going back to my initial pick, Liam. Duel’s not looking nearly as dominant here this week as he did last week. Maybe that mask was like Samson’s hair for Duel… Ump: I’m actually surprised that you’d come up with something that insightful, Derik, but regardless, I still think it’s too early in the contest to really make any kind of prediction, especially in light of the fact that Duel has currently BLOCKED the cross armbreaker by hooking his own arms, which looks to have aggravated Shabazz to no end! Ump: Duel somehow managing to roll away from Shabazz here, and he drives the black Superman to the mat. And in an amazing show of strength by Duel, he gets to his feet…Shabazz still hanging from his arms! This is just amazing! Duel makes a half-rotation in the ring here, and…I don’t believe it! I’ve never seen anything like that; it was almost like a face-first powerslam! :Shaw: Maybe I was wrong about Duel… Ump: And Duel continues his offensive, pulling Shabazz back to his feet and circling the stunned Shabazz like a mongoose circling a cobra, just looking for the right opportunity to strike and avoid the potent venom of his potential victim. Duel with a waistlock…NO…reversed by Shabazz! Shaw: The Black Superman has him here…FULL NELSON! Ump: Ah, but it’s not as simple as that, Derik, as Duel merely uses his size advantage to power out of the hold – I don’t think Jordan Shabazz’s fingers were completely locked there. And Duel with a snapmare takedown to take control again – and what’s this? Duel immediately applies a dragon sleeper, laying down on the mat and scissoring his legs around Shabazz’s waist in order to keep him from moving. This could be it… Shaw: No, no Liam, look! Shabazz has his foot on the bottom rope! Duel didn’t keep him far enough from the ropes for that move to be effective! Ump: And in a show of good sportsmanship by Duel, he releases the hold at the referee’s first request… Shaw: Yeah, he’s definitely not my style of wrestler… Ump: …and again, both men are back onto their feet. Duel with the Irish whip…no! Reversed by Shabazz into a short-arm Cracka Crippler! Duel is in the middle of the ring here, with nowhere to go. This could be the end of it! :Shaw: Yes! Yes! I knew it! Shabazz has to win this match! He’s the man! Ump: You realize that he probably doesn’t like you, right? And unlike me, he doesn’t even have a good reason. Shaw: …oh. Ump: However, regardless of the reverse racism that this man perpetrates, I’ve got to give Jordan Shabazz credit – he uses that crossface submission hold to perfection, and he can apply it from nearly anywhere. It’s just a dangerous, dangerous submission hold, as Duel is finding out as we speak. Shaw: Speaking of Dangerous, the winner of this match will be moving on to fight Max Danger in the next round. I’d love to see a Shabazz/Danger match, myself. Ump: I’m sure you would, Derik, but the fact remains that Shabazz has to beat Duel, and when he’s been in the Cracka Crippler for this long without tapping, I’m not sure what it’s going to take from Shabazz. By my count, Duel’s been in the hold for nearly two minutes now! Shaw: Pshhh…he’s going to give up any minute now… Ump: Apparently, he’s not! Shabazz has just released the hold, and from the look on his face, he’s clearly not happy. He begins to stomp away at Duel, and I’m not sure how much the big man has left – what’s this? Duel with a leg screw, but Shabazz’s body landed on the arm that was just in the crossface, and Duel isn’t able to capitalize! Shaw: Hahaha, yes! Shabazz has this match in the bag – after all, how’s a one-armed man going to win? Ump: I’d like to point out at this point that our neighbors in Connecticut recently hired a one-legged wrestler, so don’t rule anything out, Derik. Shaw: …I’m speechless. Further proof that the owner of that company is on cocaine. Ump: Back to the action, though, as Duel somehow manages to set Shabazz up for that devastating move he put Dirk Seller away with on Pressure Point 49, the stepover toehold face lock with double armbar. Shaw: Shabazz blocks with an elbow to the head! And another… Ump: …caught by Duel! Duel releases the toehold and pulls Shabazz to his feet…kick to the back of the knee! Duel applies an inverted full nelson, putting his head into the middle of Shabazz’s back…and he flips forward, using the arch of his back to bridge the hold and put even more pressure on Shabazz’s neck! And Duel’s yelling something at the referee here; let’s see if our cameras can pick it up. Duel: Ask him! Ask him! ASK HIM! Referee: Shabazz, do you want to quit? Jordan X Shabazz: No! Never! I’m not going to submit to a white man! Ump: And there you have it! This match is so much more than a contest between two rivals – for Duel, it’s a huge chance to prove himself in his new stomping grounds, and for Shabazz, it’s a testament to his very ideology! Shaw: Duel’s released the hold, and I can’t see the logic in that. Ump: For once, Derik, I agree with you. No matter what he said, Shabazz could only hold out for so long – after all, he’s only human. Shaw: Wrong! He’s the Black Superman, Liam, and there he goes again! Shabazz is just spectacular! Ump: He just low-blowed Duel when the big man tried to pull him to his feet! He would’ve been disqualified if the Reverend Jesse Kintei hadn’t been distracting the referee. What’s so spectacular about that? Shaw: The beautiful teamwork between Kintei and Shabazz! They’re definitely on the same page! Ump: This is a singles match, Derik – I think Kintei should be removed from ringside, if he continues to have an impact on things. At any rate, with newfound control, Shabazz uses it to his full advantage, pulling Duel into a front chancery…leaping brainbuster! He calls that the Cop Killa! Shaw: And it’s especially appropriate since Duel used to be a cop in Philadelphia. Ump: That’s in poor humor, even for you, Derik. Regardless, Shabazz immediately looks for the Cracka Crippler again, and you’ve gotta admire his persistence – he’s doing everything he can to put Duel away here. Oh my! In another tremendous showing of strength from Duel, he stands up before Shabazz could get the hold fully locked in! Arm draaaaaaaaaaaaaag! Shaw: Uh, what was that? Ump: I’m not entirely sure – it just came over me momentarily. At any rate, Duel continues with his momentum, using several stiff chops to the neck to send Shabazz to the mat once more…what are we going to see here? Stepover crucifix armbar with neck submission?! Regardless of how well he does in the tournament, I think Duel has really shown us that he has the makings to be the King of Submissions. Again, we’ve got some commotion in the ring! Duel: C’mon, Shabazz, you know you want to give up…just do it, save yourself the pain! Shabazz: No! I’m not giving up to you, and I’m not going to be held back by anybody who works for the man! Ump: And again, Duel simply releases the hold out of frustration. This is actually something I’ve seen from him in other tapes before – he tends to get frustrated easily, and his anger often clouds his better judgment – let’s see if that proves to be his undoing here. Shaw: He’s pulling Shabazz into an Irish whip here, Liam – I’d say he’s still in control, which pretty much sucks. Ump: Oh! It wasn’t an Irish whip at all – it was set-up for a short-arm released overhead belly-to-belly suplex…but no! Shabazz blocks with a series of headbutts, leaving him back in control! With a brutal double chop to Duel’s neck, Shabazz is really establishing himself in the late goings of this contest! Shaw: Irish whip by Shabazz, and this match is getting better by the minute! Armbar takedown, directly into the Cracka Crippler! Shabazz is the man! Ump: Don’t speak too soon, Derik – somehow, Duel has managed to get his knees beneath him, and he’s trying to stand up! Shaw: But Shabazz quickly puts the brakes on that, only releasing his hold long enough to nail Duel in the back of the head with an elbow! He’s got the Cracka Crippler locked in again! This has to be it! SHUT THE FUCKING HELL UP EVERYONE!! Ump: ... (And the Crowd pretty much did.) LLB: ::Breathing heavily, with rage mixed in with some mild tears in his eyes:: Shut up... all of you... LLB: Because... (Young grinned evilly.) LLB: It’s true. Ump: & Shaw: WHAT!?!?!?!? (A dead silence crept throughout the Arena, as the Pay-Per-View logo showed in the bottom left-hand corner, and a final shot of both Reed Young and Sean Cylear was seen.
